## Review 32

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2023-05-22 18:04:48 |
| **Guest** | Carol |
| **Reservation #** | 3368163986.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 8 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 10.0 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 10.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 7.5 |

**Title:** Great place for a short stay, especially if you need to access LAX airport easily.

**✅ Positive:**  
I liked the fact this hotel is so very close to LAX airport.  Skyways doesn't have its own shuttle service so, we took the Hertz Rental Car Shuttle which left us right across the street from Skyways. It was so easy, and it worked out great.  Our room was very clean and roomy.  There is also a very nice courtyard on the premises. There is a small, Greek restaurant right next door which looked nice. We did not have the time to try it.  The planes stopped flying overhead around 11:00 pm. No problem having quiet after that. We would most certainly return to Skyways. It's affordable, compared to many other hotels in the area.  One must go there with the mindset that this is more of a "motel", no frills establishment. It's a great value for the money. I highly recommend it.

**❌ Negative:**  
There was no telephone in our room.  It's not an issue in the day and age of the cell phone, but, it's something to be aware of if this is important to you.

## Review 41

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2023-05-25 01:26:40 |
| **Guest** | Gianni |
| **Reservation #** | 2503051443.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 7 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 7.5 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 10.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 7.5 |

**Title:** Ideale per stare vicino all’aeroporto

**✅ Positive:**  
Ho soggiornato una notte come scalo per dividere il viaggio dall’Italia alle Hawaii e questa struttura è vicinissima all’aeroporto. I prezzi sono molto competitivi rispetto ai dintorni. Le camere sono semplici ma pulite. La doccia funziona bene e proprio accanto alla struttura c’è un ristorante greco dove si mangia bene. Direi che è stata un’ottima soluzione. La signora della reception è stata molto gentile e mi ha fatto scoprire che per tornare in aeroporto non serve il taxi ma basta attraversare la strada ed entrare nel parcheggio della riconsegna delle auto a noleggio, da lì partono le navette gratuite (per sicurezza ho chiesto prima di entrare alla sicurezza e mi hanno detto che non c’erano problemi).

**❌ Negative:**  
La connessione wifi funziona poco e male. Essendo una struttura vicino all’aeroporto mi sarei aspettato una migliore insonorizzazione, ma invece la camera in cui ho soggiornato era parecchio rumorosa. Infine il ragazzo che mi ha accolto al check-in non era molto disponibile e, nel 2023, ancora non conosce bene come funzionano i pagamenti con Apple Pay o simili.

**Title:** Small, clean, affordable hotel close to LAX -- but poor wifi is a problem

**✅ Positive:**  
This is a small but clean and affordable motel property very close to LAX Airport. The rooms are clean, have mini-refrigerators, individual air con units, and comfortable beds.

**❌ Negative:**  
The current wifi setup at the motel is poor, though management say they are working to improve it in the months ahead. Connections are weak to the point of almost being inaccessible or very slow.
Also, the location is right under the flight path for landing planes at LAX, meaning there's a regular flow of pretty noisy jets coming in right over the motel, with the busiest times seemingly around the dinner-early evening hours. Though that has never bothered me in terms of hindering my ability to sleep well when staying there.

**Title:** Would stay again for the convenience

**✅ Positive:**  
Great price and location! You can take the free shuttle from the airport to Hertz and then walk over to the motel. I actually walked into a suite with two rooms and three beds, it was massive, kind of a nice surprise! Was comfortable and clean, good A/C, fridge, microwave, hair dryer, iron, pretty courtyard outside with flowers. I just started a night so it suited me

**❌ Negative:**  
The area isnt the greatest, food and shops are ten minute walk away. I checked in late and would've felt a little unsafe if there hadnt been another party outside with me checking in too. There's no key card lock on the gate to get to the rooms.

**✅ Positive:**  
Staff was amazing at 1am when we checked in. Carried my bag and stroller upstairs. They were very accommodating for our last minute reservation. Clean, comfortable bed, nice little crib set up for our toddler, right across the street from the car rental place, exactly what we needed after a long travel day.

**❌ Negative:**  
It smelled faintly of cigarettes initially, but it went away with time.

**Title:** Ne pas y passer plus d'une nuit  et ne pas avoir faim .

**✅ Positive:**  
Chambre grande et bien équipée et hôtel proche de l'aéroport .

**❌ Negative:**  
Les toilettes juste derrière la porte de la salle de bain , pas de porte aux chambres en intérieur , le quartier : que des parkings , aucun restaurant sur place et autour .
Pas de possibilité d'avoir un petit déjeuner sur place . L' hôtel est extrêmement bruyant , les avions passent juste au-dessus de l hôtel et travaux à proximité . Nécessité d'une réception plus conviviale ,  juste une personne dans un petit local derrière une vitre . On se croirait dans un parloir de prison . Pas d'accès pour personne handicapée pour l étage .

| **Guest** | Aizon |
| **Reservation #** | 2854358890.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 8 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 10.0 |
| **Location** | 7.5 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 10.0 |

**Title:** Great budget location if you don't mind the noise.

**✅ Positive:**  
The room was cleaner than expected, and the courtyard was clean and well put together. There's a gate to get into the courtyard + rooms, so it felt pretty safe. The room I stayed at had a fridge which was very convenient as I was traveling with heat sensitive items. The location is very close to the airport, so it's a good spot to stay the night for an early flight the following day. Just be aware that; due to it's location, arriving/departing aircraft can be very loud. I'm a fairly light sleeper myself, but I managed to sleep through most of the evening by wearing wireless earbuds and listening to a video or a podcast. If you don't mind the noise, then it's definitely a good spot to spend the night without having to spend a lot of money.

**❌ Negative:**  
The bedside lamp in my room didn't turn on, but it's okay as there are other ways to have ample light in the room. The only outlet for charging my phone was by the TV. It would've been nice to have one by the bed so I can charge my phone throughout the night and have it within arms reach. I also wish the curtains closed all the way as some of the lights from outside were coming into the room, I would've liked a little more privacy.

| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 10.0 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 10.0 |

**Title:** Transformative!

**✅ Positive:**  
I love patroning small businesses like hotels in Los Angeles because you REALLY get a feel for the city. Albeit visiting L.A. each year, this year I was solo dolo and felt so comfortable in navigating my way around. Skyways Hotel is VERY convenient to local restaurants and businesses, to the rental car places and LAX. The LAX tram they are building will be right behind Skyways hotel (LITERALLY) and the hotel is close to major highways and beach destinations like Manhattan Beach and Marina del Rey.  You will hear planes but it didn't bother my sleep and I am usually a light sleeper. There were many different types of people staying at the hotel and no one had complaints. I never heard one disruption or saw unsafe behavior occurring. Exploring LAX/Westchester you find a beautiful of everything. I even found a local restaurant that serves the BEST breakfast I have had for barely. $10! I enjoyed my stay.

**❌ Negative:**  
Each room comes with a fridge but not a microwave. I couldn't warm leftovers up. Heavier bed covering for those who might get cold. Laundry facility.

**Title:** Convenient, cheap, but loud.

**✅ Positive:**  
The hotel is very close the airport. It is very basic but has everything you need for a short stay (shower, towels, a desk, etc.).

**❌ Negative:**  
The noise from the airport is very loud, and the hotel isn't nearly as soundproof as I had hoped. You could hear the planes taking off at night, and that makes getting good rest predictably difficult. Also, as close as this property is to the airport, you will still have to take a taxi/Uber/Lyft to get there, which partially nullifies the point of being so close to the airport as the amount of time and money you save is minimal. Lastly, the hotel is located in a rather industrial area, so the surroundings aren't very comfortable.

**Title:** Great for the price and a short overnight stay!

**✅ Positive:**  
Airport transportation was easy taking free shuttle with the Herz car rental shuttle located across the street from this hotel.  The room and bathroom were very clean.  The gentleman who signed me in was extremely helpful and delightful.  I would stay here again and I'm an older woman traveling alone most often.

**❌ Negative:**  
I couldn't find the main entrance.  There was a gate that was open and I entered but didn't know where the main office was and I had to shout to find someone.  There needs to be a sign on that gate to show people that the main office/window is around the corner.  This part was a little scary for me as I was afraid to leave my luggage unattended and look around.   I had started to climb stairs with my luggage looking for main office when the attendant finally appeared to help me.  I didn't know it but climbing those stairs would have brought me to more rooms.

| **Guest** | Greg |
| **Reservation #** | 3371823843.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 5 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 10.0 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 7.5 |

**Title:** We only needed to stay one night given we were flying out of LAX the next day, would stay again.

**✅ Positive:**  
Easy check-in.  Owner was the check-in agent and very personable.  Easy to get from car to room. Accomodations were all accetable.  Lovely interior courtyard to sit outside if you like.  The very was very comfortable and easy to cool the room down on a hot day in LA.  Refrigerator was great.  Nice appointments with desk, etc.  It was very close to take-out and restaurants.  Very clean.  In talking with the owner he had just recently purchased it was in the process of making improvements.  He was repairing the second story roof at the time were there and encouraged us to make a review.  He was trying to make go of it as his first time investment in operating a hotel.  Kudos to him.

**❌ Negative:**  
Water pressure in tub was great, but the shower head (we used) was really poor given there was a rain showerhead.  No USB ports, but understand. Sub-par Mr. Coffee single use coffee maker.  Given it was so close to the LAX you could hear plains come and go up to midnight and start again at 6am.  The hair dryer was a goner.  It barely produced any heat and the blades were shot, rattling it spun.

**Title:** Average motel. Clean room. Aircon works great. Staff is polite.

**✅ Positive:**  
The staff (especially the owner/manager) were polite and helpful. Access to the airport and rental cars was great. Room was clean and air con worked great.

**❌ Negative:**  
I felt that the price for the room could have been less expensive considering the facilities (a price reduction per night by at least 25% seems reasonable to me). Additionally, the whole time I was there, there were roof repairs going on and the equipment used was emitting a strong diesel odor throughout the property. I understand that roof repairs need to be done, but the diesel smell did not enhance the experience. The lack of a microwave in the room was a bit unpleasant and made eating difficult. This seems like an easy fix.

**Title:** Excellent hotel if you want to stay near the airport

**✅ Positive:**  
I had an early morning flight and chose to stay the night before nearby and picked this hotel. Upon check-in, I returned my car rental and was pleasantly surprised that the Hertz car rental was right next door; dropped off the car and walked back to the hotel.  Hertz also offers shuttle service back to the airport, FREE.  There is a patio outside of the rooms where you could sit and watch the airplanes fly over.

**❌ Negative:**  
If you have a car, parking is extremely limited.

**✅ Positive:**  
Great personnel, very kind, welcoming and going above and beyond.
Rooms were clean and cozy and felt safe.
Perfect solution if you need to sleep over on budget in a somewhat walkable distance from the airport.
In the night, the airport traffic was as noticeable (and definitely not waking me up, as some others tend to describe here).

**❌ Negative:**  
Just a nitpick, but the shower valve is of an unusual type. You need to pull it to start the water flow, but it felt so weird to pull it that hard for the first time... So much so I had to ask in the reception late at night...

**Title:** Ma come si raggiunge dall'aeroporto?

**✅ Positive:**  
Motel tranquillo e piuttosto isolato, scelto per passare la prima notte a Los Angeles in attesa di ritirare l'auto al vicino noleggio Hertz.

**❌ Negative:**  
La mancanza di un servizio navetta dall'aeroporto. Infatti, purtroppo, dall'aeroporto non è stato semplice arrivare in hotel perché a differenza di quanto detto in altre recensioni l'autista della navetta Hertz (una signora davvero molto scortese) ci ha fatto scendere e ci ha detto che non potevamo usarla per arrivare in hotel anche se avevamo l'auto il mattino seguente. Siamo poi saliti su una seconda navetta e arrivati al noleggio Hertz abbiamo individuato l'uscita pedonale e raggiunto l'hotel.

**Title:** Perfekt für wenige Nächte zum Zwischenlanden.

**✅ Positive:**  
Sehr nah am Flughafen und gut zu erreichen mit dem Shuttlebus der Mietwägen-Anbieter. Es war sauber und ist perfekt für 1-2 Nächte Aufenthalt wenn man in LA zwischenlandet. Einfacher Check-in und Check-out (per Schlüssel Briefkasten, falls man früh abreisen muss).

**❌ Negative:**  
Die Flugzeuge fliegen direkt über das Hotel, bis spät in die Nacht und die Fenster sind nicht schalldicht. Aber mit Ohropax war es überhaupt kein Problem. In der Gegend gibt es nicht wirklich was zu sehen, dafür sehr nah am Flughafen.

**✅ Positive:**  
Kamers zijn erg basic, netjes overall, voldoende faciliteiten zoals airco etc. Echter verwacht geen sfeervol ingerichte omgeving. Dit geldt ook voor de omgeving rond de accommodatie.

**❌ Negative:**  
Dit is een degelijke accommodatie voor een kort verblijf. Bijvoorbeeld na aankomst of bij vertrek van LAX. Verblijf ligt vlak onder een aanvliegroute, houd rekening met geluid van landende vliegtuigen. Alle ramen dicht helpt iets, maar door zo dicht bij LAX doet dat niet zo veel. Oordopjes! 

Ik raad deze plek af voor een langdurig of meerdaags verblijf. 
Gezien de ligging, sfeer en net hier boven genoemde zaken.

Overigens het naastgelegen Grieks restaurant is erg goed!

## Review 438

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2023-10-07 15:35:38 |
| **Guest** | Paola |
| **Reservation #** | 3008385295.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 4 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 5.0 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 5.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 5.0 |

**Title:** Clean and close to airport

**✅ Positive:**  
it was cheap and not far away from the airport.
I'd like to give a 10/10 to the staff: there was a guy at the reception, when I arrived late at night, who was really nice and polite. 
Room and bathroom were clean. There is a shuttle bus to the airport (walking distance: 10 min) in the rental car parking lot in front of the hotel.

**❌ Negative:**  
it's a cheap hotel, therefore I got what I paid for. I was between 2 flights and I didn't have a big budget, so I chose this hotel. I had 2 heavy suitcases and I didn't like the fact that there was no lift to go upstairs after a 12h-journey on a plane. This is not a negative point per se, as I said, I had a tight budget and this is what I could afford. 
The room looked old and very "tired", it definitely needs a refresh. But bed sheets were clean and this was an excellent point. 
The hotel is near the airport, so expect a lot of noise from 5 am in the morning when the air traffic is back.

## Review 443

|---|---|
| **Date** | 2023-10-10 13:03:56 |
| **Guest** | Norman |
| **Reservation #** | 4036271102.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 9 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 10.0 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 10.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 10.0 |

**Title:** Clean, safe, affordable, and near LAX.

**✅ Positive:**  
I was looking for an inexpensive, clean, and safe hotel near LAX to catch an early-morning flight. This hotel fit my requirements perfectly. The accommodations are simple, but the room and the bathroom were clean and had no unpleasant odors. The area around the hotel, at night, also didn't seem overtly suspicious or dangerous. Early-morning check-out was possible by dropping my key in a box. After check-out, I was able to walk from the hotel to the LAX city bus center (about a 15-20 minute walk), then catch a free LAX shuttle bus to the airport. Supposedly there is also a free shuttle bus departing from a different property near the hotel, but I couldn't find detailed information (schedule, location, route) and opted to walk instead.

**❌ Negative:**  
There seems to be no elevator leading to the second floor, so you have to haul your luggage up the stairs.

**❌ Negative:**  
Advice from the hotel to use the free Hertz shuttle bus from the airport was not good, since Hertz have put up fences and now you have to walk all the way up to the car-return point and back down again to reach the hotel. Also shower didn't work, but it was only one night so we didn't bother getting them to put us in another room. Everything else worked.

**Title:** Scary but turned out ok.

**✅ Positive:**  
Location

**❌ Negative:**  
Sign in on the street. Person didn't tell me how to get to room. It was late at night and he didn't specify that it was on the second floor and not accessible via the first stairway by the entrance. Fortunately I only stayed a few hours since I had a very early flight.

**🏨 Property Reply:**  
Thank you for taking the time to give us your feedback.  Our building is U shaped and there are 3 stairwells and accessible all the around.  Sorry you missed loop around the hotel.  We work diligently to provide affordable clean and comfortable stay to all our guest.  Sorry to hear you did not have a good stay.  We will do better.  Jan

**Title:** Fine for a couple hours of sleep.

**✅ Positive:**  
This location was close to the airport. The bedsheets were clean.

**❌ Negative:**  
The photographer for this location needs a raise, because they made the location look much better than what it is. Vending machines were not stocked. Rooms were dingy. There was no light in the shower, so I had to shower with the curtain open in order to see anything. Unsure if the door lock to our room actually locked. Claims that it is easy to reach by taking the Car Rental shuttle, but then it took us 10-15 minutes to exit the car rental area because all of the gates were closed. Did not justify the price paid.

| **Comfort** | 10.0 |

**Title:** HINT: How to use the shuttle

**✅ Positive:**  
The building is that of a typical two-story motel. They did a good job making it comfortable. There is a patio inside and a Greek restaurant adjacent to the hotel. This is an industrial area and to find another restaurant you would have to walk a mile. Several car rentals are a walking distance from the hotel

**❌ Negative:**  
The hotel page on Booking.com doesn’t talk about a shuttle. However, in a welcome message to guests they say that you can use the Hertz shuttle. HINT: once you board the shuttle, keep silence. The driver will say that the shuttle is only for those who have a reservation for car rental. If you say that you don’t or that you go to Skyways, the shuttle driver will ask you out

| **Comfort** | 10.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 7.5 |

**Title:** It was a very nice stay, overall. Everything was fine. Liked its close proximity to LAX Airport

**✅ Positive:**  
I liked the room set up, how comfy the bed was, and the shower had both cold and hot water running just fine :) It is also close to the airport, as the Uber ride only took about 17 minutes to get to the airport and be dropped off at Terminal 3. I think the location close to LAX was what I liked the most about this place. A U.S. Postal Service is located within a 15-20 minute walk also, in case anyone needs to go there.

**❌ Negative:**  
I would say the only issue was the price, a bit pricey in my opinion for the only night I stayed there, but I would not blame the hotel for that. That is just a part of how much things costs traveling to Kali nowadays.

## Review 590

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2023-12-16 17:39:41 |
| **Guest** | Norman |
| **Reservation #** | 4231594742.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 4 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 5.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 7.5 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 2.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 5.0 |

**✅ Positive:**  
Die Lage, um schnell zum Flughafen zu kommen , ist sehr gut. Das war es dann aber auch schon.

**❌ Negative:**  
Die Dusche funktionierte nicht richtig, es gab kein warmes Wasser. Sie Toilette war sehr laut, so dass man die nicht nutzen konnte, wenn der Partner schlief.. Zumindest nicht, wenn man ihn nicht wecken möchte.
Das schlimmste ist aber, dass die Flugzeuge im Landeanflug direkt über das Gebäude fliegen und das in einer Höhe von max 15 bis 20m. Dies geschieht in etwa alle 3 bis 5 Minuten und fühlt sich an, als flöge es durch das Hotelzimmer. An ruhigen Schlaf ist nicht zu denken.
Wer es wir wir, nur als kurzen Stopp vor dem nächsten Flug nutzen will, sollte woanders hingehen oder direkt zum Flughafen, dort ist es ruhiger.

| **Reservation #** | 4014631334.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 5 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 7.5 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 7.5 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 7.5 |

**Title:** Only suitable for a short stay (10pm-4am in our case) to catch early flight & AVIS free LAX shuttle

**✅ Positive:**  
Convenient LAX location, across the street from car rental companies (and use their free airport shuttle bus), room is minimally decorated but clean and spacious, with the lowest price for the area. Limited free parking shared with the on-site restaurant (we were lucky the restaurant was closed the day we were there). The area is "human quiet" (no random unsavory folks roaming around as rest of LA).

**❌ Negative:**  
This property is right underneath the landing path, so every 5-10 minutes there are loud plane noises. Limited lights/lamps in the room. The shower did not work and staff demoed how to jiggle the handle in order for it to function after we called, but the water pressure is weak. No sound proof at all, hearing upstairs tenants footsteps and bathroom noises, definitely not meant for extended stay if you want quietness.

---

## Review 681

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2024-02-20 16:57:35 |
| **Guest** | Joumana |
| **Reservation #** | 4215464015.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 5 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 5.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 5.0 |
| **Facilities** | 2.5 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 5.0 |

**Title:** Convenient for airport but don't expect more

**✅ Positive:**  
The room was adequate and comfortable, and the hotel is conveniently close to the airport with a free Hertz shuttle stopping nearby. It was cheaper than anything else in that vicinity. Great if you just want a night near the aiport for an early flight. BUT.

**❌ Negative:**  
This is a dystopian-looking dive with zero soundproofing despite being directly under the flight path of landing planes: the noise is terrific and this really should be mentioned in the description. It is not safe, anyone can walk into the courtyard and access any of the doors; it's only protected by its dinghiness and remoteness. There is no feel of staff presence, only some poor guy in a reception cubicle that looks designed to stop disappointed patrons from reaching them. Also a few things were frankly misrepresented. Despite the hotel's claims, room is NOT cleaned daily. It looked clean when I arrived, but I was there 4 nights and by the time I was leaving I was grossed out. I booked 4 nights and was only provided with 2 doses of coffee, which was of course not replenished. The vending machine only provides soft drinks and there is no food to be found anywhere near unless you walk to the expensive hotels further down. The restaurant claimed to be on site is an uninviting taverna that only opens when it feels like it, and definitely not for breakfast. The shower wasn't working properly and there was also something wrong with the flush. So even though it was cheaper than anything else nearby, it was outrageous for what I got, and I feel I was robbed.

---

**Title:** Very good price-to-quality place to stay close to LAX

**✅ Positive:**  
I wanted to stay near the airport, as I had a morning flight, so I was OK with the airplane noise that you could hear all the time. The room was bigger than I expected, with a writing desk, TV, big fridge, bed was comfy. Very good price-quality ratio if you need to stay close to the LAX (also, there are free LAX shuttles close by).

**❌ Negative:**  
The drain in the bathroom was clogged, so dirty water was accumulating when you take a shower. Room door had to be slammed shut, otherwise it would not close completely, so I almost left it unlocked, luckily a staff member pointed this out to me. But frankly, those are not big deals, I would recommend Skyways to travellers and would visit again.

You are under the flightpath of landing planes….bring earplugs! Although in the night it stays quiet for a while. But this you know in advance, so no reason to complain.
The general maintenance needs some attention.

## Review 748

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2024-03-30 21:29:20 |
| **Guest** | SaraHanes |
| **Reservation #** | 4167257402.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 9 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 10.0 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 10.0 |

**Title:** Greta value for money and super close to the airport. Clean!!

**✅ Positive:**  
The building looks dated but it was thoroughly cleaned and there was no bug. I had read reviews before I stayed and some people recommended bringing earplugs as it is so close to the airport. To I used foamy ear plugs at night and had a really good sleep.  Some reviewers wrote that they coups not figure out how to use the shower and neither did I at first. I tried turning the know, pulling, and pushing.. i
I could not get it to work so I got my boyfriend to do it and he figured out it had to be pulled a little harder than I had done. It work3d no problem.
We used Hertz shuttle bus from LAX to the hotel and from the hitel to LAX. There are frequent services and saves you 25 dollars Uber journey.

**❌ Negative:**  
We wanted to get some water and found the vending machine outside, which sold drinks for only 1 dollar each. It only took cash and the front desk was happy to change our 5 to 1 dollar bills. We happily went up to the vending machine only to find that water was sold out.. 
I recommend bringing empty bottle from home and filling it up at the airport water fountain before coming here. It would be good if the vending machine was filled more often and it would be good if the front desk had some in stock to sell to the guests..

---

| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 7.5 |

**Title:** Good for staying here if you need to be close to the airport.

**✅ Positive:**  
Clean room with a fridge and good space, but bathroom needs renovation. Comfortable beds and very nice staff at the desk, we were welcomed with a big smile. Previous opinion said about the airplane noise, because it’s close to the airport, but it didn’t bother us that much. We heard maybe only 3 planes during one night (or maybe we were just tired ;) Got good information about shuttle and it was pretty easy to take it.

**❌ Negative:**  
Bathroom needs renovation. No big light on the ceiling, just lamps. Traffic, traffic, traffic, if you plan to visit for example Santa Maria rather than just the airport, we were stuck in a traffic for more than an hour.

## Review 766

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2024-04-09 03:21:28 |
| **Guest** | Nadine |
| **Reservation #** | 4028780341.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 7 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 7.5 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 2.5 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 5.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 7.5 |

**✅ Positive:**  
Das Hotel ist nicht weit vom Flughafen, direkt gegenüber von Hertz, Budget und Dollar Autovermietung. Wir wollten nach Ankunft 19.45 in LAX nicht noch durch die Stadt, sondern eine Nacht am Flughafen pennen, dafür ist das Hotel ideal. Mit dem Hertzshuttle ist man schnell da, dieser fährt sehe regelmäßig. Aus der Hertzanlage rauskommen ist dann nicht ganz einfach, am besten folgt man dem Weg den auch die Autos nehmen, da alles andere abgesperrt ist. 
Zimmer sind einfach, aber zweckmäßig, Preis Leistung für die Lage wirklich gut. Schneller Check in, Zimmer sauber, aber schon etwas älter/angewohnt.

**❌ Negative:**  
Hertz Shuttle wurde als Tipp angegeben, ist auch super da kostenlos, aber der Weg vom Shuttle zum Hotel gestaltete sich etwas schwierig. Mehr als eine Nacht würde ich dort nicht bleiben wollen, Zimmer sehr dunkel und trist. 2 Queenbetten im Zimmer, für 2 Leute ideal für mehr, anstrengend, da sobald sich der eine bewegt der andere auch aus dem Schlaf gerissen wird.

**❌ Negative:**  
I am a smoking person. I want to comment that it is totally NON SENSE to make smoking forbidden in the garden of the hotel. I understand no smoking policy in the rooms, it is everywhere. **Title:** Great stay, very close to LAX, great service, Highly recommend.

**✅ Positive:**  
We enjoyed staying at Skyways Hotel. 
Having access to the Hertz free shuttle service was great.  The check-in process was simple and staff helpful with pointers for us being out-of-towners.  Room was clean, functional and all in working order.  Our stay was for 2 days so the no daily servicing of the room would have what we requested even it was a service offered.  Food was great at Greek restaurant beside the hotel.

**❌ Negative:**  
Yes, the planes can be heard overhead however were not particularly noticable.  No planes operated from 1am to 4am. 
 Besides it is so close to the LAX airport.

|---|---|

| **Overall Score** | 1 / 10 |

**Title:** Wasn’t worth the $112.86 that they charged me.

**✅ Positive:**  
NOTHING

**❌ Negative:**  
Almost EVERYTHING. The place is very OLD and very DIRTY, and inside the room SMELLED very BAD. Went to open the DIRTY curtains and the rod to pull fell right off screw and all, sat on the toilet and it rocked back and forth. On one side of the bed the lamp didn’t WORK. Shower water on, off and cold, hot knob just kept spinning had to grab hold of the part sticking out of the wall and turn it. Homeless shelter is next door so a lot of sketchy characters hanging around outside. We had a early international flight and we drove in from Vegas, in a rental car had eleven pieces of luggage in the car wanted to be by the rental car return so I thought that this would be good for us. And yes that all did work great. But because of all the sketchy characters, walking around outside, I slept in my car while my wife and son, ( my WIFE  was HELLA PISSED OFF AT ME)  because I chose this place. I’ll  tell you why I slept in my car, the parking for this dump is in an alley behind the building and probably only has enough parking for 10 or 12 cars, across from that is a fence with a tarp covering it up so now one could see in or out so someone on the other side of the fence could not see anything nonsense going on. While in my car at night I could see rats running around outside. The outlet’s are all on the baseboard of the wall and also looks very OLD. The place looks like a FIRE HAZARD inside. I should of have taken some photos wasn’t thinking like that. I usually don’t have to leave a review because most places that I would stay are up to par. BUT THIS PLACE I HAD TO SAY SOMETHING, STAY AWAY.

**🏨 Property Reply:**  
Thank you for your recent stay with us.  We work diligently to offer safe, clean and comfortable accommodations to all our guests. I wish you had expressed while checking in that the hotel was not up to your expectation. We would have been happy to work with the site you booked your reservation to cancel or resolve any issues without penalty.  As one of the inexpensive option in the Los Angeles Airport area, we want to reassure all our future guest we do work hard to provide safe, clean and comfortable stay.

| **Overall Score** | 5 / 10 |

| **Location** | 5.0 |

| **Comfort** | 5.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 2.5 |

**Title:** pas top

**✅ Positive:**  
La proximité avec l’aéroport pour une nuit, on peut se rendre chez les loueurs de voiture à pied
Accueil sympa de la réceptionniste

**❌ Negative:**  
Le bruit infernal des avions qui passent vraiment très bas et jusque très tard dans la nuit
Chambre très vieillotte, salle de bains idem, serviettes de bain petites et très usagées 
Pas de petit déjeuner ou diner possibles aux alentours ni dans l’hôtel, il faut marcher 20 mn pour trouver des commerces,  et au bord d’une grande route sans rien autour, coin qui ne nous a pas paru très sécurisant. Il y avait pourtant une taverne grecque juste à côté mais fermée pour des raisons d’hygiène (panneau bien visible)
Le prix  du taxi pour y arriver 21 USD pour une course de 6 mn, montre en mains. Il n’y a pas de taxi à la sortie du terminal, il faut prendre un bus navette gratuit (bus vert) qui vous dépose en moins de 10 mn sur la,plate-forme des taxis et Uber.

**Title:** This was a cheap place to stay for a night after arriving at midnight into LAX and reflected that

**✅ Positive:**  
It was helpful to be able to take the hertz bus and walk across the street- though I suspect I got lucky with the driver not asking me for a reservation #, I might recommend this more if you are traveling to the airport where they might not ask as many questions.

**❌ Negative:**  
Luckily I was on the second floor because the front window didn't lock (none of the windows were locked when I entered, but this one wouldn't budge). The room was a bit dingy but the bathroom was clean. The socket on the lamp shorted my phone charger, leaving me without the ability to charge overnight and a bit of a stressful morning dealing with the consequences- I recommend digging behind the bedside table for a real wall plug even though it is very dusty back there.

Het binnenhofje is best gezellig, zeker door het Griekse restaurantje wat er bij zit. Het hotel zit op loopafstand van een supermarkt (+/- 15 min), wat ook fijn is.

**❌ Negative:**  
De kamers zijn best lawaaierig. Je hoort de vliegtuigen heel duidelijk overvliegen. In de nacht stopt dit wel, maar het kan de slaap beÃ¯nvloeden.

**Title:** Bien pour une nuit près de l’aéroport

**✅ Positive:**  
Pour une nuit, l’établissement est plus que convenable. Effectivement nous entendons les avions mais cela paraît logique au vu de l’emplacement. L’établissement est tout de même calme, la literie est confortable. Le personnel qui nous a accueilli était très sympathique. Je recommande pour une nuit lorsqu’il y a nécessité de dormir près de l’aéroport (attention à la distance à pied)

**❌ Negative:**  
Établissement éloigné du Terminal B de l’aéroport (40 minutes à pied)
Équipements et mobilier vieillissants, la propreté peut être améliorée (rideaux de douche notamment)

La stanza ed il bagno erano puliti, il letto grande e c'era il minimo necessario, frigobar e ferro da stiro. Il giusto solo come appoggio per dormire. Parcheggio fuori non custodito ma funzionale. Per essere LA il prezzo era ok e la posizione non è lontano da venice beach.

**❌ Negative:**  
La zona è isolata, accanto all'aeroporto. Per fortuna il rumore degli aerei ci ha lasciato dormire. Non sono venuti a pulire la prima sera.

**Title:** Muito boa

**✅ Positive:**  
Gostei da acomodação. O quarto é bom, a cama e o banheiro são legais, e o hotel fica bem próximo do aeroporto. O check-in e o check-out são muito fáceis de fazer. Para chegar ao hotel, você pode ir a pé. Achei a região bem segura, ou você pode pegar o shuttle da locadora Hertz, que fica em frente ao hotel. Eu preferi ir a pé, pois do aeroporto fui ao restaurante In-N-Out Burger, onde você pode filmar os aviões chegando em LAX, e de lá para o hotel é bem pertinho. Lembrando que o hotel fica bem na rota da pista do aeroporto de Los Angeles. Durante o dia, você ouve o barulho dos aviões, porém, até meia-noite, não se ouve mais nada, e você consegue dormir em paz. No entanto, bem cedo, o barulho dos aviões começa novamente, mas nada que incomode.

**❌ Negative:**  
Nada a reclamar

**🏨 Property Reply:**  
Thank you for taking the time to leave a review. Regarding water in room, the hotel provides bottled water in the vending machines located inside the hotel courtyard. Additionally, all rooms have running water in the toilet, sink, and shower. To utilize the running water in the toilet you simply flush, for the sink you will need to turn the faucet to begin water flow, for the shower you must twist the handle then pull to begin water flow. Should we have the opportunity to service you in the future please do call the front desk should any problems arise and an agent is more than happy to show you how to handle any issues that may arise. Safe Travels, Skyways Hotel

## Review 1166

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2024-10-24 11:35:29 |
| **Guest** | Tim Fatchen |
| **Reservation #** | 4668753591.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 8 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 10.0 |
| **Location** | 7.5 |
| **Facilities** | 10.0 |
| **Comfort** | 10.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 10.0 |

**Title:** Comfort with excellent restaurant in close proximity to LAX without the close proximity cost!

**✅ Positive:**  
Excellent and helpful communication with management and staff. Was able to arrange an early  check-in for a small fee (the Sunset Ltd arrived over an hour EARLY at 4:15AM -- sheesh). Externally looked a bit unimpressive but oh, a lovely garden/courtyard. Proximity to airport was original main concern. And wow: Aliki's Restaurant attached was a Class #1 discovery: ethnic Greek food done properly, even retsina available! Mundane food also available but why bother with hamburger when the souvlaki + pita bread is to die for? (but closed Sundays) Hotel room was ample, coffee maker and coffee/sugar etc (bring your own tea), beds comfy, bathroom good, excellent blackout curtains and reasonable soundproofing.

**❌ Negative:**  
No getting away from it being close to the airport on a major runway approach. But we found that although the planes were noticeable the soundproofing was enough for them to sound like a quickly passing heavy truck, and we got to the point of not noticing. Also, planes are curfewed (6:30 AM startup, 11:00PM end (? or thereabouts)

---

| **Guest** | Dave |

| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 2.5 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 2.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 5.0 |

**✅ Positive:**  
The bed

**❌ Negative:**  
Te staff didn't want to help me with an early check in .  After a 15 hour flight I had to wait 5 hours outside before I could check inn. Staff not helpful.  Thanks to the owner I could check inn at 11 am. I was waiting since 6 am. Everything was closed at that hour so I had to wait in the cold.  I had to walk like 6 to 7 blocks to get to a starbucks. At 10.30 am I started to walk back to the hotel.

**🏨 Property Reply:**  
Thank you for the review Dave. After taking a look at your reservation it seems you requested the check in at 7am however we noted it was based on availability. Availability means that a room has been checked out and cleaned. Unfortunately, arrives at 9am as most guests are not check out at 6 am. The reason behind your delayed check in was due to the room needing to be cleaned. Thank you.

**Title:** A Bit of a Disappointment

**✅ Positive:**  
The hotel is close the airport, offers free parking and the hotel is within walking distance to rental car companies (Hertz, etc.)

**❌ Negative:**  
When I stayed at this hotel before the rooms were decent. Room #139 needs to be renovated. It looks like someone slammed the door knob into the wall 100 times. The wall caved in (or maybe there was a hole there) and the job to repair it was sub-standard. It looks like the dent in the wall was not re-plastered, but just painted over. There is also a loose light switch near the front door. The area near the door needs to be painted.

The toilet doesn't seem secure.

**Title:** bien situé pour qui veut être près de l'aéroport et bon rapport qualité-prix

**✅ Positive:**  
la localisation près de l'aéroport est la raison pour laquelle j'ai choisi l'établissement. Chambre énorme et salle de bains raisonnable. Rien d'exceptionnel en termes d'aménagement mais ok et accueil sympathique.

**❌ Negative:**  
la déco est assez déprimante et le système de bain-douches avec robinet dont le débit et la chaleur sont liés est très vorace en eau. très basique dans l'ensemble, même si la taille même de la double chambre (il y a de la place pour bien plus que deux personnes!) met à l'aise. Pas de petit déjeuner inclus, pas même d'endroit prévu pour manger, j'ai dû faire un bon km de marche pour prendre mon petit déjeuner...

|---|---|
| **Date** | 2024-12-26 16:41:16 |

| **Location** | 5.0 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 2.5 |

**Title:** Would not recommend or stay here ever again.

**✅ Positive:**  
It was close to the airport.

**❌ Negative:**  
The location was so unsafe and the parking is a nightmare. When we arrived there were no parking spots. The front desk man told us that it was likely people eating at the next door restaurant and to wait it out… we had to park in the back at night with 2 small children where a homeless man was living and it smelled like urine. When walking to the front, we realized most of the cars there were Uber and Lyft drivers waiting for rides. It’s annoying the hotel doesn’t ensure their guests have somewhere to park and have those people towed.
The doors had a huge gap at the bottom that made the sound of the airplanes passing overhead unbearable. There was hardly any table/counter space to place my things off of the floor. 

Our stay here was a nightmare.

The room we were assigned was newly decorated but the doors and floors were in horrible condition. White towel on the floor that must have been there for shoes..(not sure) when you get into bed the springs in the mattress squeak line they are rusty metal.
(Thin walls)The room next to us was sooo noisy..after midnight we expected quiet to try and grab some sleep.

**Title:** Mostly positive experience on site marred by very poor invoice follow-up.

**✅ Positive:**  
Respectful, direct on-site staff; comfortable bed, clean bathroom (if a bit skimpy on toiletries). Presence of 24-hour on-site staff. Pleasant courtyard setup, e.g., plants, tables, awnings, chairs, etc. Good on-site parking.

**❌ Negative:**  
((1)Wet toils on counter and floor after shower not replaced for next day; (2) entry door hard to open (had to call staff to help open "stuck" unit entry door; (3) entry door key failed at 2 separate times (had to visit staff office to have key reset); (4) Didn't receive invoice as promised at checkout until several calls (and several days) to office (with staff telling me at first that email address that they sent invoice to was sent to completely incorrect email address (I had clearly indicated my email address at registration). I needed to call Booking.com several times who also was initially unable to contact the hotel administration by phone fvoice message on hotel answering machine requested " code" which Booking was unable to ident9ify) and who sent strongly worded email to hotel (copied to me) which after a few days resulted in an invoice (and apology to me and Booking.com) about their lack of appropriate follow-through). This invoice story marred an otherwise fairly pleasant stay at the hotel.

**Title:** Old, run down hotel

**✅ Positive:**  
The location was ok but that’s it.  The sheets were clean or appeared clear. The place is old, rundown, and filthy.

**❌ Negative:**  
The property is due for serious repairs and upgrades.  The walls have cracks and uneven drywall.  There are no hooks in the bathroom and the place just looks filthy.  Unfortunately I do not recommend staying here until the owner does a complete renovation and upgrades to the floors, tvs, chairs, and drywall.

**🏨 Property Reply:**  
Thank you for taking the time to leave us a review. As a small budget hotel by LAX, we aim to offer all guests a cheap and clean stay while traveling to Los Angeles. We are sorry to hear we did not accomplish this and will speak with our team to ensure an issue like this does not occur for future guests. Should we have the opportunity to provide you service again, please let us know at any time if there are issues with the room and a front desk employee will happily assist with any accommodation issues. Thank you.

**Title:** Everything but the airplane noise was fine.

**✅ Positive:**  
The hotel and room were VERY old. But the room was clean and satisfactory.  The bed was comfortable.  There were convenient plugs on the nightstand to charge electronic devices.  The wifi was fast. Parking was super convenient and free.

**❌ Negative:**  
The location of the hotel was convenient to the airport.  HOWEVER, the hotel is as close to the airport runway as possible and the planes take off every couple of minutes.   The windows are old and single pane glass.  So it was very, very loud in the room.  So loud you couldn't hear/understand what was being said on the TV when a plane took off.  The planes at LAX come and go at all hours, so it was hard to sleep.

Muy dejado. Acolchados manchados. Aroma muy polémico. Toallas muy pequeñas. Muy ruidoso (sin aislamiento en las ventanas). El precio vs la calidad es inadecuado. Ademas no tiene shuttle. Con lo cual a los supuestos 100 dolares la noche hay que sumarle 60 mas de ida y vuelta a la terminal. No tiene ninguna opción de comida ni bebida

**Title:** Basic and a bit run-down, Skyways Hotel is okay for one night on a tight budget, but don’t expect co

**✅ Positive:**  
Close to the airport.

**❌ Negative:**  
We stayed at Skyways Hotel just for a quick shower and change before our connecting flight — and honestly, that’s all it’s good for.

The room was dark and a bit gloomy, with a dated and uninviting bathroom. The property used to be part of a motel chain and now feels quite run-down. The surrounding area isn't great either - even our Uber driver mentioned she hesitated before picking us up there.

One thing that felt a bit off: during check-in, I was offered a $10 discount for paying in cash. I didn’t have any, and although I was pointed to an ATM, I ended up paying normally by card. The push for cash payment felt a little shady.

Technically, it’s within walking distance from the airport (about 20–30 minutes), which we did after a long flight. But there's really nothing in the area, aside from a Greek restaurant right next door.

Bottom line: if you’re traveling on a tight budget and just need a place to crash for one night, it’ll do — but not more than that.

---

**Title:** Good for a short stay near LAX airport.

**✅ Positive:**  
Good for a short stay near LAX airport. Took the free Hertz rental car shuttle, about 15min wait and 10min journey without traffic, 20min with traffic, then approx 5min walk. Room itself was a decent size, had the basics and was acceptably clean. Rooms on 2nd floor are up a flight of stairs, did not see a lift. Some noise from heavy vehicles along road outside and planes taking off/landing at airport. Did not bother me, but to take note of if you are a light sleeper.

**❌ Negative:**  
Room door had a very basic lock, would have appreciated a secondary chain lock or similar.

**Title:** This hotel is convenient and handicapped-accessible but very noisy.

**✅ Positive:**  
It is very close to the airport. The staff was helpful: they were going to put us on the second floor but don’t have an elevator, so because we had very heavy luggage, immediately changed us to a ground floor room. The room was obviously made to be handicap-accessible, as it was roomy and the shower was large and open, with safety features and a seat. The air conditioning worked well, and the room had a mini fridge. The property has a security gate.

**❌ Negative:**  
It was VERY noisy. Not only is it in the airport’s flight path, the water pipes, I guess from the upstairs room(s), were so loud that they woke me up.

**Title:** Not anything nice, but cheap and easy for a layover!

**✅ Positive:**  
Convenient to airport and cheapest stay around. Staff was friendly. I used the free Hertz shuttle across the street to get to and from the airport (I was nervous the workers would notice I wasnt a customer but  no one paid any attention). There is a big fence surrounding the Hertz lot and it was kind of hard to make my way out but I just asked an employee and they showed me (just walk through the car exit gates).

**❌ Negative:**  
The place is quite run down and you can hear everyone who walks by your room. The area is pretty desolate around the airport. I was a little concerned about safety as a solo female when I arrived at night, but it was fine. I even went on a run in the morning and felt fine.

## Review 1658

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2025-08-20 15:24:49 |
| **Guest** | Ana Paula |
| **Reservation #** | 4550249717.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 9 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 10.0 |

**Title:** Ótimo custo benefício para quem vai a Los Angeles e não se importa em ficar no centro

**✅ Positive:**  
Próximo ao aeroporto e as locadoras de veículo. Tem estacionamento gratuito no local. A cama é bem confortável e o quarto tem um tamanho ótimo. Oferece amenities, como sabonete e shampoo/condicionador. Toalhas limpas. 

O checking e checkout foram fáceis e os atendentes são cordiais! 

Há várias conveniências perto, o que ajudou muito para lanches rápidos. 

É possível ir a vários pontos turísticos rapidamente de carro e também há ponto de ônibus de fácil acesso em frente ao hotel.

Considero um ótimo custo benefício para quem quer ir para Los Angeles e não pagar fortunas em estadia, com um conforto simples.

**❌ Negative:**  
Única reclamação é a pressão da água do chuveiro. Poderia ser um pouco mais forte. Além disso, a torneira do chuveiro era bem dura para ligar e ficamos com medo de quebrar. Mas fora isso, tudo certo!

---

## Review 1660

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2025-08-21 00:32:03 |
| **Guest** | Siobhan |
| **Reservation #** | 5006629803.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 3 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 2.5 |
| **Cleanliness** | 5.0 |
| **Location** | 7.5 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 5.0 |

**Title:** I saw Skyways Hotel in a Different Light this Trip

**✅ Positive:**  
The air conditioner was nice, the bed was large and comfortable, the TV service was decent, the water pressure in the shower was great, the shower water warmed quickly and the mini refrigerator in the room was sufficient. Hertz and Thrifty Rental car companies are across the street. You can take the LAX Airport shuttle to the Hertz and Thrifty Rental car offices and just walk across the street to the hotel or just pickup and drop off a rental car. The hotel has about 3 street parking spots and maybe 8 free parking spots behind the hotel - including 1 spot for guests with a disabled parking permit. The two male hotel front desk employees were nice.

**❌ Negative:**  
Normally I stay one night alone. This time I stayed 4 nights with my husband. The room only had 3 hangers. I asked for 8 more. The woman at the front desk did not want to give us more then said that she would ask someone. We never got the extra hangers and could not hang our clothing. The room only had 2 large towels, 1 hand towel & 2 face towels for two adults staying 4 days. Housekeeping never visited our room so after 4 days we had 4 bags of trash & 1 bag of recycling piled next to the door. The room would have been fine for the price with a repainted bathroom door and some attention to the entertainment center in the main room. A flap of material was sticking out. My husband found the sound of planes too loud in Room 130. Every room should come with a microwave.

---

**Title:** GREAT value airport hotel.

**✅ Positive:**  
Great value for an airport hotel in LA. Very close to the airport, also just one block away from the new public transit interchange station. They even let me store a bag after check out for the rest of the day so I could do some sight seeing before a late flight. The Greek restaurant next door is a good choice for an evening meal in a hurry.

**❌ Negative:**  
Being so close the airport it is a bit noisy with planes landing frequently. They don't go all night but do go fairly late. However expected this.

**✅ Positive:**  
1. Excellent Location and Accessibility
Prime Proximity to LAX: The hotel's location is a significant advantage, situated less than two miles from Los Angeles International Airport (LAX). This makes it an ideal choice for travelers needing quick and convenient airport access.
Convenient Initial Shuttle Service: The frequency and directness of the shuttle service from the Hertz rental car facility were excellent, providing a swift and easy transfer upon arrival.

2. Exceptional Customer Service
Friendly and Helpful Staff: The entire hotel staff was consistently courteous and professional. Their pleasant demeanor contributed positively to the overall guest experience.

**❌ Negative:**  
​1. Transportation & Logistics
​Hertz Shuttle Departure Experience: While the hotel's proximity to the Hertz facility and the shuttle frequency were appreciated, the instructions for departing the Hertz location were inadequate. I spent over 15 minutes attempting to exit the bus driveway due to a lack of clear signage or direction for pedestrians. Clear guidance on leaving the facility is necessary.

​Lack of Return Shuttle: The absence of a return shuttle service to the airport or rental car facilities is a significant inconvenience. I was required to use a ride-sharing service, costing approximately $25, which negatively impacted the value proposition of the stay.
​
2. Room & Amenities
​Non-Functional Shower: The shower in the room was completely non-operational (no water pressure whatsoever). This required me to use the face wash sink for basic cleanup, which is unacceptable for a standard hotel stay. This fundamental issue needs immediate attention.
​
Concerns about Pests: Although the bed appeared to be in good condition, I woke up with several insect bites on my hands and legs. While I cannot confirm the source, I strongly recommend a thorough inspection of the bedding and mattress to rule out any potential issues with bed bugs or other pests.

**🏨 Property Reply:**  
Thank you for the stay and feedback. It is with great concern that I am responding to your experience during your stay at our hotel. Hearing of this through a review after your departure, . Today we have completed an inspection of  the room and want to assure you and all our guests that there was nothing found.  We work diligently inspecting daily and have a professional exterminating company perform monthly services to provide a safe, clean and comfortable stay for all our guests.  Also we have noted your comments and shared them with our management team for review. All required steps are being initiated to ensure that these issues are resolved to the highest standards. We hope you will return to stay with us again.

---

**Title:** Unsafe property with terrible cleanliness standards

**❌ Negative:**  
The property is marketed as a hotel but the facilities don’t match this description. The room had chipped paint, exposed cinder blocks, stained bedsheets and unsealed electrical fixtures. The lock on the room was flimsy and inadequate. The room was on the ground floor and an open gate to the property would have allowed public access to the bedroom door. This property has serious safety issues. I asked for a wake up call before a flight and the receptionist asked if I had a phone and told me to set an alarm on it.

**🏨 Property Reply:**  
Thank you for your stay at our hotel.  We are sorry to hear about your experience. We strive to make sure all our guests have a clean and comfortable stay.  If you arrive and feel that the hotel is not up to your expectation, please let us know and we are happy to work with the booking site to resolve the issue amicable.

**Title:** Emplacement idéal pour une connexion à Los Angeles. Personnel très gentil.

**✅ Positive:**  
La Navette gratuite Herzt vous y emmène en 10 min depuis l’aéroport et il y en a toute les 10 min environ. Il faut juste arguer que l’on a une réservation mais que la connexion mobile ne marche pas pour la prendre.
Le personnel est très aimable, il m’a gardé en conceirgerie mes sacs toute la journée, et j’ai même eu droit à une chambre gratuite pour me changer en fin de journée avant de repartir. 10/10.
Il y a un distributeur de boisson et snack mais qui ne fonctionne uniquement avec du cash.
Escaliers uniquement pour monter ses valises si vous êtes au 1er étage.
Propre et fonctionnel.
Le personnel vous envoie des messages prévennant et est à votre écoute.

**Title:** Dangerous cesspit of filth and depravity

**✅ Positive:**  
Absolutely filthy cess pit right next to a homeless hotel with the rudest housekeeping staff imaginable. The literal toilet cleaners—who should be quietly and respectfully cleaning the toilets—spy on the guests and harass them. Absurd. Very low IQ interactions all around. Definitely do not stay here. The windows don’t even lock—there were vagrants right outside my window screaming all day and I feared they might break in. You cannot walk around outside in this area if you are a normal, not insane and drug addled, person. I have no doubt this place is probably the home of numerous crimes. I should have just spent the money and stayed somewhere safe. I have literally felt safer in a backpackers hostel in New Delhi. This place is an insane asylum.

**❌ Negative:**  
Dangerous cesspit of filth and depravity

**🏨 Property Reply:**  
It is with great concern we are responding to your review. After your initial stay at the property, our records show you made a second reservation to extend your stay at the hotel. Our attempt to address your concern was responded to with derogatory comments. We will work with the booking agent you are using to make reservations, to resolve this matter. Thank you.

2) Send Housekeeping and Management come to speak with you in person to offer new linen and address anything else that would help you feel safer. 

Please ensure we are having the manager come to speak with you, right away, to ensure what would make the biggest difference for you today (Room change, extra security, refunds, anything at all.) Thank you again for giving us insight and the chance to make this right.

Best regards, Skyways Hotel Management.

## Review 1979

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2026-02-26 04:12:33 |
| **Guest** | Anita |
| **Reservation #** | 6934264011.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 8 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 10.0 |

**Title:** Recommended if on a budget, and day noise doesn't bother you. Bring earplugs. Pleasant owners.

**✅ Positive:**  
I'd like to give this older motel some love. It is a modest establishment, yet clean. Everything worked and we felt secure. Our room was not updated a great It had a pleasant courtyard and a terrific Greek restaurant attached. The land lady let us check in a little early and gave us directions to In and Out burger. 15 minute walk. A MUST SEE for planespotting and eating  a burger. She was very nice. We picked this place because it is walking distance to airport, our flight was at 7 and this was important. It was 35 minutes with suitcases. I suppose you could catch the Hertz shuttle across the street at a better hour than 4am when we left. I liked the location, it is walking distance from the Metro station too to catch subway train. We came from San pedro post cruise and didn't leave until early the next day. Pricewise, it was very good compared to surrounding places to stay. We stayed February 14 and some of the prices in LA were eyewateringly high.

**❌ Negative:**  
Due to its location being close by to one of the main runways, it is very loud with planes landing during the day. I brought ear plugs and suprisingly had a decent night sleep. It must slow down after 10pm. Not the fault of the motel. If this motel was updated a little more this would definitely be a hidden gem. But I would stay again.

## Review 2005

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2026-03-09 15:36:00 |

| **Reservation #** | 5068121551.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 7 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 10.0 |
| **Cleanliness** | 5.0 |
| **Location** | 10.0 |
| **Facilities** | 5.0 |
| **Comfort** | 7.5 |
| **Value for Money** | 10.0 |

**✅ Positive:**  
地理位置很好，在洛杉矶机场可以坐去Hertz 租车的shuttle bus大约十几分钟然后步行到酒店，在马路对面。员工服务热情，我们早上十点到达下午五点退房事先和酒店邮件联络，没有收取提前入住费用。可以作为机场中转的酒店，性价比高。酒店旁边有个希腊餐厅，早上十一点开始营业。

**❌ Negative:**  
酒店设施非常简单，房间有些许霉味，飞机声不绝于耳。旁边没有任何商店除了那个希腊餐厅，酒店庭院里的自动售卖机是坏的，连水都买不了。建议在机场买点食物再入住。

## Review 2035

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| **Date** | 2026-03-23 14:19:13 |
| **Guest** | Luca Grementieri |
| **Reservation #** | 6440880522.0 |
| **Overall Score** | 8 / 10 |
| **Staff** | 7.5 |
| **Cleanliness** | 7.5 |
| **Location** | 7.5 |
| **Facilities** | 7.5 |
| **Comfort** | 5.0 |
| **Value for Money** | 7.5 |

**Title:** Buono per una notte di scalo

**✅ Positive:**  
L'hotel è facile da raggiungere usando lo shuttle gratuito fino alla metro e poi comminando per 10 minuti. La stanza era grande e con una comoda macchina da caffè con cialde.

**❌ Negative:**  
Il bagno era molto piccolo e angusto, soprattutto in relazione alla grandezza della camera e al numero di posti letto. L'hotel è molto vicino all'aeroporto, questo è un pregio per passarci una notte di scalo, ma implica anche un forte rumore causato dagli aerei a tutte le ore. Con un sonno leggero è impossibile dormire.
Inoltre abbiamo trovato la camera poco mantenuta, con le prese di tutte le lampade inavvertitamente staccate.

**Title:** Passabel wenn man auf der Durchreise ist und am nächsten Tag das Flugzeug nehmen muss.

**✅ Positive:**  
Nähe zum Flughafen, der zu Fuß 15 bis 18 Minuten erreichbar ist, ist gut. Daher gute Wahl, wenn man am nächsten Tag zum Flughafen muss. Passabel für eine Nacht wenn man auf der Durchreise ist. Die Metro ist zu Fuss in 10 Minunten erreichbar.

**❌ Negative:**  
Die Flugzeuge fliegen genau über dem Hotel, d.h. es ist sehr laut und ab 6h Früh schwer zu schlafen. Keine Restaurants, Cafes, oder Supermärkte im Umkreis. Es gibt nur einen Getränkeautomat. Auch kein Frühstücksangebot im Hotel (oder im Umkreis), dh keine Möglichkeit sich vor Ort zu verpflegen. Ohne Auto daher schwierig. Zimmer etwas dunkel.

**Title:** Buena opción si llegas en auto propio.

**✅ Positive:**  
El lugar está cuidado, limpio , con buen olor.

**❌ Negative:**  
Que no tenga desayuno, que los aviones se escuchan, no está insonorizado. 
Que al momento de la reservación según ellos existía una rentadora de auto que ofrecia traslado las 24 hs desde el aeropuerto y que podía usarse gratuitamernte para llegar hasta allí.  Pero casualmente el mismo día que tengo que hacer el check in me indican que la rentadora se mudo y que ahora, el transporte gratuito del aeropuerto era lo más cerca y me dejaba a 15 min caminando.
Una falta de respeto al cliente no entregar esa información en tiempo y forma ya que si hay que adicionarle un Uber al costo.l la tarifa ya resulta similar a cualquiera de los hoteles de 4 estrellas situados en la avenida principal.

**Title:** タクシーの件から、2度と利用したくない

**✅ Positive:**  
値段は地域で1番安かった。

**❌ Negative:**  
ホテル街やメイン道路から離れていて(周りに何もない)、交通手段がタクシー以外に無い。
また、8時のフライトの為、6時までに空港に行きたかったので、余裕をもって、朝5時にフロントのスタッフにタクシーを呼んでもらった。しかし、呼んでもらったタクシーはいっこうに来ず、フトントまで戻ってスタッフに何度か話したが、「お前がタクシーを見逃したんだ」と言われた。さらに何度かやりとりしたが、気づけば6時になり、らちがあかないので、結局、空港までスーツケースを持って40分歩いた。タクシーぐらいちゃんと呼んでほしいのと、空港近くのホテルなのにスタッフに時間感覚がないので低評価をつけさせていただきました。
